Plan, route, write.\\n\\nInvestigation requests are read-only. They produce a cited explanation or a recommendation, not a code change. Read the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md) and this playbook before applying it. Preserve its Investigation routing for how, why, are-we-sure and choice-between-alternatives questions.\\n\\nBefore source reads or delegation, discover [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md) and follow [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md) and the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Use only exposed, authorized read operations. Inherit parent model and effort unless a supported explicit selection is observed. Requested models are not observed models. The routed skills own their prompt and source contracts; this playbook directly invokes no script helper.\\n\\n1. Route through the [how skill](../../how/SKILL.md). For motivation questions, also route through the [why skill](../../why/SKILL.md). Read their complete instructions and the prompts and source playbooks required by the chosen route. Keep every investigator and explainer read-only, including connector-backed reads. Inspect delegated findings before relying on them. If independent workers are unavailable or unauthorized, disclose reduced capability and apply the same roles sequentially. Name inaccessible, denied, failed and searched-empty sources separately. Limit the answer to inspected evidence. Missing required companions or no authorized relevant source reads blocks the dependent answer.\\n2. Throughput checkpoint stays one line: `throughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only investigation`.\\n3. Produce the how-shaped output, Overview / Key Concepts / How It Works / Where Things Live / Gotchas, omitting sections only as how permits. For a decision between alternatives, produce a recommendation with a tradeoffs table. For motivation, preserve why confidence tiers, source coverage, contradictions and unknowns within the investigation output. Cite actual inspected sources. Accessible sources that support the answer are enough to deliver it without starting a product build or prototype.\\n4. Apply the [unslop skill](../../unslop/SKILL.md) to the reply.\\n\\nNo PR, no babysit, no [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) unless the investigation precedes a code change. If it does, hand back to the user and re-route to [Bug fix](bug-fix.md) or [Feature](feature.md). That handoff does not authorize implementation, commits, PR creation or other writes. The Investigation exception takes precedence over the mode index generic Opening a PR rule. Do not install tools, change credentials or modify source state to obtain evidence.\\n\\nReply with the investigation output. For are-we-sure answers, include your real judgment with reasons. Push back if the premise is wrong under the mode Autonomy rules. Broad autonomy never overrides user authorization or host permissions. Disclose unperformed reads, uncertain conclusions and reduced roles; structural checks establish no runtime behavior or independent-worker success.\\nconst entries = new Map();\\nexport function put(key, value) { entries.set(key, value); }\\nexport function get(key) { return entries.get(key); }\\nexport function invalidate(key) { entries.delete(key); }\\nimport { createDisposer } from './vendor/disposer-v1.mjs';\\nimport { get, put, invalidate } from './cache.mjs';\\nexport function mount(key, report) {\\n  put(key, { ready: true });\\n  const subscription = createDisposer(() => report(get(key).ready));\\n  return () => { subscription.dispose(); invalidate(key); };\\n}\\n// Exact vendored fixture dependency implementation, pinned by local SHA-256 in manifest.json.\\nexport function createDisposer(onDispose) {\\n  return { dispose: () => queueMicrotask(onDispose) };\\n}\\nCompleted unit: scoped cache input copied with original bytes. Decisions: preserve cache code; investigate teardown before proposing changes. No cache fix or external delivery is claimed.\\nPending: inspect cache teardown ownership.       "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/source-reads.raw.json",

      "owner": "local-013-initial-resume-revision",

      "text": "[\n  {\n    \"path\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/session-pickup.md\",\n    \"content\": \"### Session pickup\\n\\n**You own the resume point. Read the prior trail, don't redo it.**\\n\\nUse [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md) to observe history, file reads, execution and persistence separately. Follow the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md) for revision, ownership and interrupted actions, and [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md) for supported inherited selection. A transcript reference does not prove access or continuity. This playbook grants no new permissions.\\n\\n1. Locate the prior trail. Use a transcript in the active workspace's explicitly identified agent-transcripts directory, an accessible cloud-agent URL, or the supplied pushed branch. Read only the scoped trail. Never search unrelated workspace histories or private chats. Read the metadata overview and last messages first, then scan back for decision points. Read [principle-guard-the-context-window](../../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md) in full. Parse a long transcript with an authorized independent worker only after observing its binding and inspecting its result. Otherwise read bounded sections sequentially, retain a reduced timeline in the main thread and label reduced capability. Missing history or denied access leaves the reconstruction provisional. Name the missing input; do not invent prior turns or broaden access.\\n2. Reconstruct operational state. Recover the branch and worktree, original goal, what already landed, open todos and decisions. Record the prior full input SHA and assignment or action identities when present. Inspect actual current branch, worktree, full HEAD SHA, git log and git diff against the recorded base through exposed authorized read or execution tools. Compare the trail with these observations before acting. The prior trail is authoritative input for prior decisions, but a self-report does not prove current state. Reject stale assignment results whose revision or identity does not match. Preserve concurrent work; do not reset or overwrite it. If the base, revision or artifact is inaccessible, state the gap and stop dependent actions.\\n3. Diff done vs pending. Compare what shipped against what was planned, name the resume point, and retain completed work. Do not re-run the prior repro or redo completed work merely to verify from scratch. Inspect only evidence needed to reconcile current state and verify inherited claims. Resolve a changed revision or contradictory artifact before continuing dependent work. Preserve inherited authorization, explicit gates and pending decisions. For an interrupted external action, inspect its stable identity, intended destination and actual prior outcome. Unknown outcomes block replay. Recovery requires explicit authorization, destination inspection and observed safe idempotent recovery under the assignment contract; a retry policy does not establish success.\\n4. Route remaining work to the matching playbook in [poteto-mode](../SKILL.md). Pick the verdict: continue execution, ship a finished recommendation, ratify or override a prior conclusion, or postmortem a failed run. Read the routed playbook in full before dependent work. If it is missing, name that dependency and stop the route. The pickup playbook ends here. The routed playbook owns the rest, including its permission and review gates.\\n5. Verify inherited claims against the original goal on the real artifact using [principle-prove-it-works](../../principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md), read in full. A passing prior self-report is not proof. Retain actual inspection evidence and name unverified outputs. If a durable continuation record is authorized and needed, save it in the scoped owned location and verify readback. Without observed persistence, report a session-local reduced handoff; do not claim saved state, unattended recovery or cross-session continuity.\\n\\n**Reply:** where the prior agent stopped, what you inherited vs redid, ideally nothing redone, the resume point, and the outcome. Include the actual revision comparison, route or verdict, evidence and any history, permission or persistence limits. Distinguish verified completion from a provisional recommendation.\\n\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"path\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md\",\n    \"content\": \"---\\nname: principle-guard-the-context-window\\ndescription: \\\"Apply when context is filling up: large outputs, long files, repeated reads, fan-out planning. Route bulk to subagents; keep summaries in the main thread, not raw payloads.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Guard the Context Window\\n\\nThe context window is finite and non-renewable within a session. Every token should be worth its cost.\\n\\n**Why:** Context overflow degrades reasoning quality, creates compression artifacts, and halts progress.\\n\\n**Pattern:**\\n- **Isolate large payloads.** Route verbose outputs, screenshots, and large documents to subagents. The main context gets summaries, not raw data.\\n- **Keep frequently used content inline.** Templates and references used on every invocation belong in the skill file, not in separate files that cost a read each time.\\n- **Size phases and cap scope.** Limit files per phase, set turn budgets, account for mechanism costs.\\n\\n## Capability and permission boundaries\\n\\nUse the [session capability contract](../../references/host-capabilities.md) to check evidence access and independent-worker operations. Delegate only when the task authorizes it and the session exposes those operations. Follow the [assignment and integration contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md), retain observed worker identities and inspect their results before reporting summaries. This principle grants no additional permissions.\\n\\nIf independent workers are unavailable or unauthorized, narrow accessible evidence into bounded sequential phases. Keep summaries in the main thread and label this reduced capability. Sequential work does not establish worker independence. If required evidence is inaccessible, name the missing input and access limit; keep the plan provisional and do not claim to have read or summarized it.\\n\\nPreserve the invocation metadata above without assuming the host enforces it. Report delegation and inspection only after observing the authorized operations.\\n\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"path\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md\",\n    \"content\": \"---\\nname: principle-prove-it-works\\ndescription: \\\"Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. Verify against the real artifact (run the feature, read the actual value, inspect the diff), not a proxy, self-report, or 'it compiles.'\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Prove It Works\\n\\nVerify every task output by checking the real thing directly. Do not infer from proxies, self-reports, or \\\"it compiles.\\\"\\n\\n**Why:** Unverified work has unknown correctness. Indirect verification (file mtimes, output freshness, agent self-reports, cached screenshots) feels cheaper than direct observation. Acting on a wrong inference costs far more than checking the source.\\n\\nCheck the real thing, not a proxy:\\n- Check process liveness directly, not indirectly through derived state\\n- Read the actual value, not a cached or derived representation\\n- When verification fails, suspect the observation method before suspecting the system\\n\\n## Script the check when you can\\n\\nThe strongest proof is a deterministic script that re-runs the same comparison, not a one-time eyeball. Write the script, run it, and keep its output as an artifact a reviewer can re-run instead of trusting your word.\\n\\nKeep the artifact visible for the human. Commit it only for large or complex work where the trail has to be auditable later, like a big port or migration (the **show-me-your-work** skill).\\n\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\n\\nUse the exposed tools and observed operations described in [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Run checks and retain artifacts only within the user's authorized scope and host permissions. If direct inspection or execution is unavailable or denied, state which outputs remain unverified and withhold completion for those outputs. A proposed script is not evidence that it ran.\\n\"\n  }\n]"
    },

    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/working/disposable/cache/probe.mjs",
      "bytes": 395,
      "sha256": "fa8cdce0fe97a6e3ed1b071aceab4365938b862c335cfccdb94508173a7fd002",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-resume-revision",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { mount } from './caller.mjs';\nlet error;\nprocess.once('uncaughtException', caught => { error = caught; });\nconst unmount = mount('fixture-key', ready => console.log('disposed ready:', ready));\nunmount();\nawait new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 5));\nconsole.log(error ? `teardown error: ${error.name}: ${error.message}` : 'teardown completed');\nprocess.exitCode = error ? 1 : 0;\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/working/disposable/cache/invalidation.diff",
      "bytes": 365,
      "sha256": "0f2f4bee7dba00d4cb422f289fe8a6213fd891f08026892e65839113253752b7",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-resume-revision",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "diff --git a/cache.mjs b/cache.mjs\n--- a/cache.mjs\n+++ b/cache.mjs\n@@ -1,4 +1,4 @@\n const entries = new Map();\n export function put(key, value) { entries.set(key, value); }\n export function get(key) { return entries.get(key); }\n-export function invalidate(key) { setTimeout(() => entries.delete(key), 0); }\n+export function invalidate(key) { entries.delete(key); }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/working/disposable/cache/README.md",
      "bytes": 509,
      "sha256": "b6a51fa497d6924aa1d0cbbbf066c7630ea27601027424b994e24c3f8e293551",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-resume-revision",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "Review invalidation.diff against the after-state cache.mjs and the real caller outside the diff. The vendored disposer is the exact locally pinned dependency, not a mock pretending to prove a public dependency. Determine whether synchronous invalidation races the deferred disposal callback. probe.mjs runs this actual supplied implementation and intentionally exits nonzero if teardown throws. A before-state copy may restore the removed setTimeout line to compare ordering. No execution result is supplied.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/working/disposable/cache/caller.mjs",
      "bytes": 309,
      "sha256": "9566a520d6763d359c4b43cd5705df6333c8bf4a4afb96aec7ac3ca2e2145490",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-resume-revision",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { createDisposer } from './vendor/disposer-v1.mjs';\nimport { get, put, invalidate } from './cache.mjs';\nexport function mount(key, report) {\n  put(key, { ready: true });\n  const subscription = createDisposer(() => report(get(key).ready));\n  return () => { subscription.dispose(); invalidate(key); };\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/resume-revision/outputs/working/disposable/cache/manifest.json",

      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "{\n  \"scope\": \"exact local vendored fixture only\",\n  \"dependency\": {\n    \"path\": \"vendor/disposer-v1.mjs\",\n    \"version\": \"fixture-v1\",\n    \"sha256\": \"d33e8d36600ad5df008657e478e39f9100a347098b4176f37923e5056cb3427f\"\n  },\n  \"publicPackageClaim\": false\n}\n"
    },
    {

      "text": "const entries = new Map();\nexport function put(key, value) { entries.set(key, value); }\nexport function get(key) { return entries.get(key); }\nexport function invalidate(key) { entries.delete(key); }\n"

    {

      "text": "// Exact vendored fixture dependency implementation, pinned by local SHA-256 in manifest.json.\nexport function createDisposer(onDispose) {\n  return { dispose: () => queueMicrotask(onDispose) };\n}\n"

Source: `outputs/bug_why-capture/prior-return.txt`, “What I Searched,” “Contradictions,” and “Gaps.”\\n\\n### What We Don't Know\\n\\n- Whether search-clear previously worked, when behavior changed, or which revision introduced a defect.\\n- The browser reproduction, runtime mechanism, production impact, and author intent. The code ordering and recorded error converge on that explanation. No before-state comparison was executed.
